# D2L Brightspace REST route coverage

> Inventory source: D2L official [HTTP Routing Table](https://docs.valence.desire2learn.com/http-routingtable.html), with per-operation source links below. The route table currently enumerates **876** method/path pairs across seven product groups.

## Coverage status

- Official route entries inventoried: **876**.
- Corsair route wrappers registered: **874**.
- Routes with operation-specific input/output handling: **47** audited JSON/binary schemas and **24** multipart/form/resumable-upload transports (JSON resource schemas may still be generic).
- Routes with generic route wrappers and incomplete body/response models: **800**.
- Routes requiring undocumented form-field work: **3**.
- Explicitly excluded: **2** legacy browser-auth redirects described below.

Each route row below records the official method/path, path and query parameters, JSON parameter type names, request/response modes, response summary, status codes, version range, scope, and D2L source. API version discovery operations (`d2l0001`–`d2l0004`), documented binary-output routes, and selected user identity/profile routes have operation-specific Zod schemas. The remaining JSON resource shapes and three form routes with incomplete field details still need route-by-route schema work before claiming full surface implementation.

## Explicit exclusions

| Route | Reason | Official evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `GET /d2l/auth/api/cancel` | Legacy interactive ID-Key authentication redirect/cancel flow; not a normal resource request/response operation and not used by the OAuth 2 auth-code integration. | [Operation docs](https://docs.valence.desire2learn.com/res/apiprop.html#get--d2l-auth-api-cancel), [legacy auth flow](https://docs.valence.desire2learn.com/basic/legacyauth.html) |
| `GET /d2l/auth/api/token` | Legacy interactive ID-Key authentication redirect/cancel flow; not a normal resource request/response operation and not used by the OAuth 2 auth-code integration. | [Operation docs](https://docs.valence.desire2learn.com/res/apiprop.html#get--d2l-auth-api-token), [legacy auth flow](https://docs.valence.desire2learn.com/basic/legacyauth.html) |
